Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [108] [Reactive Flux Matching: Mechanism Discovery and Adaptive Sampling of Rare Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06295)
*Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes,Nicholas M. Boffi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flux Matching框架，通过从反应轨迹数据中学习当前速度和标量势来推断主导反应路径和数据驱动的反应坐标，无需底层动力学知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径采样方法能够生成连接亚稳态的反应轨迹集合，但从这些数据中提取机理洞察仍然具有挑战性，现有的基于提交概率的方法在投影到非马尔可夫集体变量时存在局限性。

Method: 提出Flux Matching框架，通过最小化反应路径集合上的二次泛函（类似生成建模中的流匹配损失），学习两个互补对象：(1)当前速度u(z)，其流线可追踪主导反应路径；(2)通过反应电流的加权Helmholtz-Hodge分解获得的标量势h(z)，作为数据驱动的反应坐标。该方法不需要底层动力学或平稳分布的知识。

Result: 与基于提交概率的方法不同，u和h在投影到非马尔可夫集体变量时仍然保持良好的定义；其水平集为改进采样方法提供了自适应界面；在分子系统上验证了当前速度轨迹生成和速率常数计算的有效性。

Conclusion: Flux Matching为从反应轨迹数据中提取机理信息提供了一个理论严谨且实用的框架，能够生成反应坐标和主导反应路径，并与增强采样方法结合以提高采样效率。

Abstract: Path sampling methods generate ensembles of reactive trajectories connecting metastable states, but extracting mechanistic insight from these data remains nontrivial. We introduce Flux Matching, a framework that learns two complementary objects directly from reactive trajectory data: a current velocity $u(z)$, whose streamlines trace the dominant reaction pathways, and a scalar potential $h(z)$, obtained from a weighted Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition of the reactive current, that serves as a data-driven reaction coordinate. Both minimize quadratic functionals over the reactive path ensemble, analogous to the flow matching loss in generative modeling, and require no knowledge of the underlying dynamics or stationary distribution. Unlike committor-based methods, $u$ and $h$ remain well-defined under projection onto non-Markovian collective variables, and their level sets in turn provide adaptive interfaces for improved sampling with enhanced sampling methods. Flux Matching is validated through the generation of current velocity trajectories and rate constant calculations on molecular systems.

</details>


### [109] [Tangram: Unlocking Non-Uniform KV Cache for Efficient Multi-turn LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06302)
*Hyungmin Kim,Minsoo Kim,Hongseok Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tangram系统，通过确定性预算分配、头组页面管理和提前负载均衡三项技术，解决非均匀KV缓存带来的系统效率问题，实现最高2.6倍吞吐量提升且保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮大语言模型服务中KV缓存的线性增长对GPU内存和带宽造成巨大压力。非均匀KV压缩虽然能保留更多信息，但引入了内存碎片化、调度复杂性和内核利用率降低等系统挑战，导致现有LLM服务系统效率显著下降。

Method: 提出Tangram系统，采用三项核心技术：(1)确定性预算分配，根据注意力头的内在模式分配静态内存占用，消除动态调度开销和预填充停顿；(2)头组页面管理，将具有相似保留需求的注意力头聚类，使用独立的向量化页表管理，最大化物理内存回收；(3)提前负载均衡，利用静态预算配置确保均匀GPU利用率且无运行时开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有基线相比，Tangram将吞吐量提高了最多2.6倍，同时完全保持模型准确性。系统实现已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: Tangram成功解决了非均匀KV缓存的系统效率问题，使非均匀KV压缩在实际LLM服务中变得可行，显著提升了多轮对话服务的性能表现。

Abstract: Multi-turn Large Language Model (LLM) serving is critical for consistent user experiences, yet the linear growth of the Key-Value (KV) cache imposes significant pressure on GPU memory and bandwidth. Non-uniform KV compression effectively preserves more information by considering the individual importance of each KV cache. However, such KV cache heterogeneity introduces various systemic challenges - including memory fragmentation, scheduling complexities, and diminished kernel utilization - which collectively lead to significant inefficiencies in existing LLM serving systems.
  To overcome these challenges, we present Tangram, a novel serving system designed to make Non-uniform KV caches practical. Tangram addresses systemic inefficiencies through three core techniques: (1) Deterministic Budget Allocation assigns a static memory footprint to each head based on its intrinsic pattern, entirely eliminating dynamic scheduling overhead and prefill stalls; (2) Head Group Page clusters attention heads with similar retention demands and manages them with independent, vectorized page tables, thereby maximizing physical memory reclamation; and (3) Ahead-of-Time (AOT) Load Balancing leverages static budget profiles to ensure uniform GPU utilization without runtime overhead. Experimental results show that Tangram improves throughput by up to 2.6x compared to existing baselines, while fully preserving model accuracy. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/aiha-lab/TANGRAM.

</details>


### [110] [Plug-and-Play Guidance for Discrete Diffusion Models via Gradient-Informed Logit Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06303)
*Hongkun Dou,Zike Chen,Fengji Li,Hongjue Li,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GILC框架，这是一种即插即用的方法，通过将预训练去噪网络重用为变分代理来高效估计引导信号，实现了无需额外训练的离散扩散模型可控生成，在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型可控生成方法面临计算开销大或需要重新训练的问题，高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定性也限制了引导方法的有效性，因此亟需一种既高效又稳定的可控生成框架。

Method: 提出梯度引导的Logit校正（GILC）框架，将预训练的去噪网络重用为变分代理来高效估计引导信号；引入无雅可比矩阵机制直接校正干净预测logits，避免高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定性；该方法同时支持可微分和不可微分的奖励函数。

Result: 在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上的大量实验表明，GILC无需额外训练即可达到最先进的性能，在多个任务上经常优于需要微调的方法，证明了该方法的高效性和有效性。

Conclusion: GILC成功解决了离散扩散模型可控生成中的计算效率和训练成本问题，通过创新的梯度引导logit校正机制，在不增加训练负担的情况下实现了优异的可控生成性能，为离散域的可控生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Controllable generation with discrete diffusion models is often hindered by high computational overhead or the need for retraining. In this paper, we present \underline{\textbf{G}}radient-\underline{\textbf{I}}nformed \underline{\textbf{L}}ogit \underline{\textbf{C}}orrection (\textbf{GILC}), a plug-and-play framework that efficiently estimates guidance signals by repurposing the pretrained denoising network as a variational proxy. To circumvent the gradient instability inherent in high-dimensional discrete spaces, we introduce a Jacobian-free mechanism that directly corrects the clean prediction logits, facilitating stable and effective guidance. Our method accommodates both differentiable and non-differentiable reward functions. Extensive experiments across DNA, protein sequence, and molecular generation tasks demonstrate that GILC achieves state-of-the-art performance without additional training, frequently outperforming fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [111] [Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320)
*Gizem Yüce,Giorgos Nikolaou,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATWU框架，通过保留目标的冲突程度来无监督识别遗忘特定token，在TOFU和RWKU数据集上实现了最优的遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法要么忽略遗忘样本中token相关性的异质性，要么依赖辅助模型、启发式规则或外部标注来估计每个token的遗忘相关性，缺乏一种原理性的方法来识别哪些token真正需要被遗忘。

Method: 提出一种基于保留冲突的遗忘特定性定义：当最小化某个token的遗忘损失不与保留最优性冲突时，该token具有遗忘特定性。将问题形式化为模型参数和token权重的联合优化问题，并设计ATWU框架，通过隐藏状态上的简单线性评分器联合学习token遗忘特定性和模型参数，无需外部token级监督。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU基准数据集上，ATWU实现了最先进的遗忘-保留权衡，优于样本级方法、基于概率的token加权启发式方法和基于辅助模型的方法。学习到的分数与真实遗忘特定片段显著对齐，表明ATWU能够识别语义上有意义的token级遗忘信号。

Conclusion: 保留冲突为识别语言模型应该遗忘的内容提供了有效准则，使能够以最小计算开销直接从模型表示中无监督学习token级遗忘特定性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model while preserving its general capabilities. For autoregressive language models, not all tokens in a forget sample are equally relevant to forgetting. Existing approaches either ignore this heterogeneity or rely on auxiliary models, heuristics, or external annotations to estimate each token's relevance for forgetting. We instead characterize it through the interaction with the retain objective: a token is forget-specific to the extent that minimizing the forget loss on that token does not conflict with retain optimality. We formalize this perspective as a joint optimization problem over the model parameters and the token weights and show that, under a natural separation condition, the resulting objective recovers the oracle forget-specific token support. Motivated by this formulation, we introduce Alternating Token-Weighted Unlearning (ATWU), a lightweight framework that jointly learns token forget-specificity and model parameters during unlearning using a simple linear scorer over the hidden states, without external token level supervision. Across TOFU and RWKU, ATWU achieves state of the art forget-retain trade-offs, outperforming sample-level methods, probability-based token weighting heuristics, and auxiliary-model-based approaches. Moreover, the learned scores align substantially better with ground truth forget-specific spans, indicating that ATWU identifies semantically meaningful token level forgetting signals. Overall, our results suggest that retain conflict provides an effective criterion for identifying what language models should forget, enabling unsupervised learning of token level forget-specificity directly from model representations with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [112] [PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328)
*Ziwen Kan,Wugeng Zheng,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAMF框架，通过先验感知的流匹配和权重共享机制，显式处理多模态时间序列中的两种缺失模式，并实现插补与下游任务的联合优化，在多个医疗基准测试中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中的多模态时间序列数据常存在不完整观测问题，表现为模态内缺失和模态级缺失两种结构不同的模式。现有方法通常通过掩码或缺失嵌入隐式表示未观测数据，无法学习实例特定的缺失信息，且大多数仅针对单一缺失模式设计。现有插补方法对不同结构先验的缺失模式统一处理，且插补过程与下游任务分离，无法引导插补生成更有信息量的表示。

Method: 提出PAMF框架：1）采用先验感知的流匹配方法，使用类型特定先验初始化流匹配源状态以区分两种缺失类型；2）通过架构匹配的编码器和权重共享机制连接插补和分类任务，将与任务相关的表示传递到插补过程中，实现插补与下游预测的耦合。

Result: 在多个多模态医疗时间序列基准测试中，该方法在不同数据集和缺失设置下相比现有基线方法实现了最强的整体下游任务性能。

Conclusion: PAMF框架成功解决了多模态时间序列数据中不同缺失模式的显式处理问题，通过耦合插补与下游任务实现了更优的表示学习和预测性能，为医疗领域的不完整数据处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In healthcare, multimodal time series tasks often operate on incomplete observations in practice, for example when ECG segments are lost because electrodes detach or an entire respiratory channel is unavailable during overnight monitoring. Such missingness typically appears in two structurally distinct patterns: within-modality missing, where values are absent within an otherwise observed modality, and modality-level missing, where an entire modality is unavailable. Existing methods typically represent unobserved data implicitly through masks or missing embeddings, without learning instance-specific missing information, and most are designed for only one missingness pattern. A natural approach is to explicitly estimate the missing data; however, existing imputation methods treat missingness uniformly despite their different structural priors, and the imputation process is often isolated from downstream tasks, preventing downstream tasks from guiding imputation toward more informative representations. To address these limitations, we present PAMF, a multimodal time-series framework that explicitly handles different missingness patterns while coupling imputation with downstream prediction through prior-aware flow matching and weight sharing. Specifically, the method initializes the flow-matching source state with type-specific priors to distinguish two missing types. It further connects imputation and classification through architecturally matched encoders with weight sharing, transferring task-relevant representations into the imputation process. Experiments on multiple multimodal healthcare time-series benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves the strongest overall downstream performance across diverse datasets and missing settings compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [113] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过代数恒等式和截断SVD近似，将高维数据局部平均曲率计算的复杂度从O(m^4)降至O(k²m+mkp²)，实现50-300倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于k近邻补丁的局部形状算子计算方法需要显式构造矩阵H，其迹形式导致每个点的计算成本为O(m^4)，使得该方法对于特征数超过几十个的数据集不可行。因此需要开发可扩展的局部曲率估计方法，使曲率成为机器学习任务中实用的几何特征。

Method: 提出两项互补贡献：(1)推导精确代数恒等式，利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性，消除矩阵H的显式构造，将特征分解后每点成本降至O(m²)；(2)用截断SVD替代完整特征分解，基于Haar测度下零空间特征向量外积的期望值推导解析近似，总成本降至O(k²m+mkp²)。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，相比原始实现获得了50到300倍的加速，且快速估计器在替代原始版本时损失可忽略不计。该方法建立了可扩展的数据驱动的局部曲率估计。

Conclusion: 通过提供可扩展的数据驱动的局部曲率估计，所提方法确立了曲率作为广泛机器学习任务（从经典方法到现代深度学习流程）中实用几何特征的地位，为几何感知机器学习算法的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [114] [Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333)
*Seyed Arshan Dalili,Mehrdad Mahdavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准稀疏自编码器(SAE)因假设特征为一维而导致的特征分裂问题，并提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器(SASA)，通过学习解码器子空间而非单一向量，将样本复杂度从指数级降至多项式级，在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上以约一半的token预算达到或超越标准SAE性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器为每个潜在特征分配单一解码器方向，隐含假设特征为一维结构。然而，大语言模型的真实特征往往具有多维结构(维度d_i≥2)，这种不匹配会强制特征分裂成多个近共线潜在单元，产生虚假多重性并模糊内在几何结构。作者旨在从几何和优化两个角度证明该问题的存在性与必然性，并设计能够原生表示多维特征的架构。

Method: 提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器(SASA)：(1)用学习的解码器子空间替代单一向量解码器；(2)通过Top-s组门控强制块稀疏性；(3)使用核范数正则化自适应调整每组的有效秩。理论证明当块大小r≥d_i时，单个组不仅能表示整个特征切片，而且是SASA目标的全局最小化器。

Result: 理论层面：证明了单方向解码器重建维度d_i≥2的特征到误差ε所需的原子数量对d_i呈指数增长，且存在从真实的d_i维基到ℓ_1正则化SAE目标更低风险的连续下降路径；SASA将样本复杂度从指数级降至多项式级。实验层面：在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上，SASA减少了特征分裂和吸收现象，提升了单调语义性和可解释性，在约一半的token预算下匹配或超越标准SAE性能。

Conclusion: 多维特征结构是SAE特征分裂现象的根本原因，子空间感知架构通过原生表示多维特征实现了特征整合，为大规模语言模型的可解释性研究提供了更高效、更准确的理论框架与技术手段。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for mechanistic interpretability in large language models, yet their formulation assigns each latent feature a single decoder direction, implicitly assuming features to be one-dimensional. We show that this assumption mismatches with the multi-dimensional structure of model features, provably inducing feature splitting through two distinct mechanisms. Geometrically, reconstructing a feature of intrinsic dimension $d_i \ge 2$ to error $\varepsilon$ with single-direction decoders forces a number of atoms that is exponential in $d_i$. From an end-to-end optimization perspective, this splitting is not merely possible but actively preferred. We prove that there exists a continuous path from the true $d_i$-dimensional basis to a strictly lower risk of the $\ell_1$-regularized SAE objective, whose descent directions drive any trained dictionary into that exponential regime. A single coherent feature is therefore fragmented across many near-collinear latents, producing spurious multiplicity and obscuring the intrinsic geometry. Motivated by this, we introduce Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders (SASA), which replace single-vector decoders with learned decoder subspaces, enforce block sparsity via Top-$s$ group gating, and adapt each group's effective rank with a nuclear-norm regularizer. We then show that once the block size satisfies $r \ge d_i$, a single group not only can represent the entire feature slice but is the global minimizer of the SASA objective. This consolidation yields a sample complexity polynomial in $d_i$ rather than exponential -- a decisive advantage given that every training activation costs an LLM forward pass. Empirically, on GPT-2 and Mistral-7B, SASA reduces feature splitting and absorption, improves monosemanticity and interpretability, and matches or exceeds standard SAEs while training on roughly half the token budget.

</details>


### [115] [Quantifying the Privacy of Counterfactuals by Leveraging Membership Inference Attacks Against Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06334)
*Maryam Babaei,Yingke Wang,Hadrien Lautraite,Heber H. Arcolezi,Ulrich Aivodji,Sebastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了反事实解释存在的隐私风险：攻击者可以仅利用释放的反事实集合（无需访问模型）成功实施成员推断攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释常用于高风险决策领域来解释机器学习模型，但通过反事实解释模型决策可能被对手利用来对模型或其训练数据进行隐私攻击。作者基于反事实类似于合成数据可作为真实训练数据替代品的类比，研究反事实是否存在类似合成数据的隐私漏洞。

Method: 借鉴针对合成数据开发的攻击方法，研究了针对反事实的成员推断攻击的有效性。具体地，设计了两种攻击场景：1）使用为合成数据设计的成员推断攻击；2）提出仅使用一组反事实（无需访问生成它们的模型）即可实施成功的成员推断攻击的方法。

Result: 实验证明针对合成数据设计的成员推断攻击在各种类型的反事实上都能成功实施。更重要的是，即使没有模型访问权限，仅使用反事实集合也能成功进行成员推断攻击。

Conclusion: 模型开发者在向不同用户发布反事实时应更加谨慎，因为反事实的发布可能导致隐私泄露，存在安全隐患。

Abstract: Counterfactuals are typically used in high-stakes decision areas to explain a machine learning model by showing how changes to the user profiles result in the desired outcome. However, explaining the model's decisions through counterfactuals can also be exploited by an adversary to conduct privacy attacks against the model or its training data. Drawing on the analogy that counterfactuals provide realistic substitutes for real training data, similar to synthetic data, we demonstrate in this paper how it is possible to successfully perform privacy attacks on counterfactuals by drawing on the attacks developed against synthetic data. More precisely, we investigate the effectiveness of the membership inference attacks designed for synthetic data on various types of counterfactuals. Additionally, while existing membership inference attacks against counterfactuals usually require to be able to query the model, we show how it is possible to perform successful membership inference attacks using only a set of counterfactuals, with no access to the model from which they are generated. Our results demonstrate that model developers should be more cautious when releasing counterfactuals to various users, as it can lead to a privacy breach.

</details>


### [116] [Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335)
*Shuxuan Li,Zhilin Zhao,Quyu Kong,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型和基础模型的预测来构建可靠的标签替代，显著提升了分布偏移下的模型性能估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能估计方法仅依赖给定模型的输出，在分布偏移时模型偏差会被放大，导致与真实性能的相关性减弱。因此需要构建更可靠的标签替代指标来准确反映模型行为。

Method: 提出融合参考对齐预测（FRAP）方法：(1) 利用外部基础模型和基础模型的互补优势；(2) 通过温度缩放校准对齐两个模型的预测分布；(3) 基于置信度加权融合对齐后的预测，构建结合基础模型鲁棒性和基础模型领域专业知识的参考分布；(4) 通过测量基础模型预测与参考分布的一致性来估计性能。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构上的广泛实验表明，FRAP在分布偏移场景下相比代表性性能估计方法提供了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: FRAP通过融合基础模型和基础模型的预测构建了更可靠的标签替代，有效解决了分布偏移下性能估计的可靠性问题，为实际部署中无标签测试集的性能预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Performance estimation under distribution shift aims to predict how a model behaves on an unlabeled test set whose distribution differs from the training data, a scenario that requires reliable indicators that can faithfully reflect model behavior without ground-truth labels. Existing approaches rely solely on the outputs of the given model whose biases are amplified once the distribution shifts, weakening the correlation with the true performance. Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [117] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等变神经置信传播(ENBP)框架，通过SE(3)等变高斯混合模型消息传递，解决了空间变量概率推断中各向异性不确定性和多模态分布表示问题，在分子构象预测和多机器人推断任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变网络仅能产生标量和向量，无法生成各向异性不确定性所需的二阶精度张量；单分量消息传递会将多模态能量景观坍缩为物理上无意义的平均值，无法有效处理SE(3)对称性约束下的空间嵌入变量概率推断问题。

Method: 提出等变神经置信传播(ENBP)框架，消息采用SE(3)严格等变的高斯混合模型；通过等变外积合成二阶精度矩阵，经可微分谱分解处理，并使用与SE(3)可交换的贪心KL混合缩减策略保持计算可行性。

Result: 在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上实现98.9%构象覆盖率，误差仅0.090Å，延迟低于1秒，速度比扩散基线快100倍以上且精度更高；在多机器人推断中，普通环状BP在15+智能体时发散，而ENBP收敛且碰撞率接近零，等变误差达机器精度(~10^-7)，优于增强基线(10^-1)。

Conclusion: ENBP成功实现了SE(3)严格等变的高效概率推断，突破了现有方法在精度张量表示和多模态分布处理上的瓶颈，为分子构象预测和多机器人协作等空间推断任务提供了高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [118] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了机器学习天气预报模型GraphCast在巴西四个气候分区的表现，发现其技能呈区域依赖性：在南半球冬季对快速移动的斜压系统预报能力较弱，而在南半球夏季湿季则能有效捕捉大尺度水汽输送并抑制对流变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习天气预报模型（MLWP）在全球范围内展现出卓越的预报能力，但全球南方地区的区域基准测试仍然匮乏，导致这些模型在复杂的高对流环境中的有效性很大程度上未经验证。

Method: 利用可扩展的云原生流程和WeatherBench-X框架，以ECMWF IFS HRES为基准，评估GraphCast在巴西四个不同气候分区的表现。选择对流层变量（T850、Q850、Z500），在四个选定季节窗口内进行分析，并使用业务IFS分析作为真值计算统计指标。

Result: 结果显示GraphCast具有区域依赖的技能特征：在南半球冬季，GraphCast在预报南巴西快速传播的斜压系统时（提前2-7天）对Z500的预报表现欠佳，但在延伸预报时段因内在平滑混沌小尺度变异性而恢复优势。相反，在南半球夏季湿季，GraphCast准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时内在抑制高频对流变异性，改善确定性数值天气预报的温度预报质量。

Conclusion: 这些发现确立了巴西地区的基准线，定义了指导未来"热带化"工作的具体物理边界，旨在优化这些基础AI模型以增强区域韧性。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [119] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图强化学习的方法，用于优化角球战术配置，能够发现新的、可推广的球员站位和策略，而非仅模仿历史模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的足球战术机器学习方法主要关注刻画历史动作或分析人员指定的反事实场景，局限于模仿已观察到的模式。作者希望超越这种历史模仿，发现新的、可推广的球员配置和策略。

Method: 以角球战术为研究对象，将其建模为决策问题：中央策略调整进攻球员的位置和速度以最大化首次触球射门概率。提出了一种基于图结构数据的强化学习架构，能够学习到一个通用策略，用于调整任意初始球员位置，而非针对孤立场景求解。

Result: 在超过3000个英超角球数据上评估，所提方法在相同推理预算下显著优于基准优化技术，能够有效发现比传统方法更优的战术配置。

Conclusion: 图强化学习可以将定位球分析从历史评估和模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现，为足球战术创新提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [120] [End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06364)
*Dexiong Chen,Till Hendrik Schulz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphDETR，一种将子图检测建模为集合预测问题的深度学习框架，通过图神经网络编码和Transformer解码器实现一次性联合预测所有模式出现位置，支持近似匹配，在大规模图上检测多达50节点的复杂模式达到91.2 AP100性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测是跨科学领域的基础问题，传统组合方法基于子图同构问题（NP完全），限制了其在小型模式或中等规模图上的应用，且只能处理精确结构匹配，无法处理近似匹配场景。

Method: 提出GraphDETR深度学习框架：1) 使用图神经网络对目标图进行编码；2) 采用固定数量的可学习查询向量，通过Transformer解码器进行解码；3) 将子图检测建模为集合预测问题，通过二分图匹配进行端到端训练，实现单次前向传播联合预测所有模式出现位置。

Result: GraphDETR能够检测多样化模式（分子结构、环、团及模糊模式，节点数达50个），在节点数达1000的目标图中有效运行；在ChEMBL数据集的分子官能团检测任务中达到AP100=91.2的强性能。

Conclusion: GraphDETR成功将DETR的集合预测思想引入子图检测领域，突破了传统组合方法的NP完全限制和精确匹配约束，证明了深度学习方法在大规模图子图检测任务中的有效性和扩展性，为近似匹配提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.

</details>


### [121] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LRFIO（学习响应场惯性算子），一种基于增量学习的HEC-RAS 2D水面高程代理模型，在四个基准测试中实现了最高2.75×10⁴倍加速，最大验证遗憾仅为4.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于栅格的代理模型存在重映射误差和信息混杂问题，需要开发直接在原始非均匀计算单元上操作的求解器一致性代理模型，以避免这些缺陷并提高预测准确性。

Method: 提出LRFIO（学习响应场惯性算子），一种无强迫、基于增量的学习代理模型：从HEC-RAS求解轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，通过闭式原生单元展开进行部署。采用基础案例优先的响应层次结构（持久性→全局校准惯性→分段响应场惯性），将分段、残差校正和神经化惯性作为可学习建模选择，仅在验证证据证明其成本合理时才保留增加的复杂性。

Result: 在四个HEC-RAS 2D基准测试中，LRFIO针对不同域保留不同的响应结构，展现了自适应学习复杂性。选择器审计显示最大验证遗憾为4.30%。部署时保留的展开时间范围为0.003-0.242秒，Beaver Bayou实测求解对比显示相比HEC-RAS有约2.75×10⁴的视界归一化加速。

Conclusion: 当前原生单元增量是一个强有力的求解器条件预测框架，额外增加的响应场、神经或空间复杂性应仅在经验验证合理时才被保留，证明了自适应学习复杂性的有效性。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [122] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对右删失生存结果的评分规则框架，通过将预测分布映射到删失机制，使得标准评分规则适用于删失数据，并提出了删失环境下多元生存建模的学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适当的评分规则为概率预测的训练和评估提供了严格的理论基础。然而，在存在右删失的情况下，事件时间只能被部分观测，导致传统的评分规则无法以标准形式应用。

Method: 核心方法是一个简单的思路：首先将预测分布通过删失机制进行映射，然后在诱导的观测数据分布上应用基础的适当评分。这种方法产生两类评分：对于固定删失时间的局部化评分，以及当删失时间随机或仅部分观测时的边缘化评分。该构造在一个连贯的框架内恢复了熟悉的右删失似然和IPCW类型准则，同时产生了CRPS、pinball损失、Brier评分和能量评分的右删失版本。

Result: 证明了在条件独立删失下边缘化评分是适当的，并且在可识别区域上是严格适当的。实验表明，在多种删失机制下，所提出的评分能够正确地对真实预测进行排序，而依赖于预测的插件加权评分可能出现排序逆转。删失engression相比在删失结果上的朴素训练有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该框架为右删失生存结果的评估提供了理论基础完整且实践有效的方法，成功将经典评分规则扩展到删失场景，并提出了改进的多元删失生存建模方法。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [123] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有关系学习评估中忽略数据集内在几何特性的系统性偏差，提出了一种基于曲率的分层评估框架CurvBench，发现模型性能表现具有几何依赖性，并在不同曲率区间内排名显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关系学习的评估实践主要依赖平坦排行榜，通过在异构数据集上平均性能来进行比较，这种方法隐含地假设所有数据集具有统一的结构。作者发现这种假设会引入系统性偏差：它掩盖了依赖于几何特性的性能变化，可能导致对模型泛化能力的错误结论。因此需要识别并量化数据集内在几何特性对模型效果的影响。

Method: 提出了一种基于曲率的分层评估框架（CurvBench），将数据集按照曲率属性划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区间。在14个数据集上评估了18个代表性模型，包括图卷积网络（GCN）、图基础模型（GFM）和表格学习方法。通过对比传统聚合指标与基于几何属性的分层指标，揭示了几何特性对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验发现模型排名在每个曲率区间内高度稳定，但在不同区间之间发生显著变化，表明模型性能具有本质的几何依赖性而非普遍可迁移。此外，识别出了某些曲率区间内，图基础模型相比几何对齐的图神经网络（GNN）呈现收益递减现象。传统聚合指标掩盖了这些关键的性能权衡关系。

Conclusion: 数据集的内在几何特性是影响模型效果的关键潜在因素，基于曲率的分层评估能够揭示传统评估方法所掩盖的性能权衡。作者提出了一种几何感知的评估协议，能够提供比标准聚合基准更可靠和可解释的模型比较结果，并开源了相关代码、数据集划分和评估工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [124] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双重预条件优化方法，通过结合梯度和激活预条件来缓解测试时反馈问题，提升了自回归任务中的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习应用（如自回归语言建模、流式生成建模、机器人策略学习）采用单步预测损失训练，但在部署时沿自身预测进行展开，导致训练验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在不匹配，且这种差距随任务长度增长。现有方法主要从数据管理、架构和目标设计角度解决此问题。

Method: 提出双重预条件优化范式，结合梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon）和激活预条件（如KFAC），针对测试时反馈挑战进行定制。激活预条件作为即插即用的干预手段，可与现有优化器配合使用。

Result: 在多种测试时反馈场景下，激活预条件的引入显著提升了下游模型性能。然而，测试性能的提升并不一致伴随验证损失的改善。

Conclusion: 双重预条件为缓解测试时反馈中的误差累积提供了新的优化设计维度。研究结果揭示了使用单步监督目标训练的模型评估方法存在根本性问题，需要重新思考评估范式。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [125] [Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06440)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统贝叶斯网络优化方法在因果推断中的局限性，本文提出了基于最大熵的推断方法，生成与数据一致的因果图谱集，揭示了优化DAG中存在的不真实因果伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络构建技术依赖优化方法，而真实数据可能存在多条因果链，导致优化方法难以准确学习因果关系；需要更能忠实反映数据内在变异性的因果表示方法。

Method: 提出基于熵的推断方法，生成与潜在数据一致的合理因果关系图谱集。在2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型的模拟噪声数据上，采样最大熵图集成，量化潜在因果关系中的结构模糊性。

Result: 实验证明"优化"的有向无环图（DAG）可能包含因果伪影，这些伪影在同等精度的拓扑结构中不一致，而最大熵方法能够揭示数据的内在结构模糊性。

Conclusion: 基于熵的推断方法能够生成更忠实于数据的因果关系图谱集，为理解复杂系统中的因果结构提供了更可靠的框架，避免了传统优化方法产生的误导性因果关系。

Abstract: Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.

</details>


### [126] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文学习的MIL方法，通过在合成数据上预训练Perceiver架构模型，实现了在少样本场景下无需梯度更新即可完成新任务的推理，在12个MIL基准测试上超越了需要任务特定训练的监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多示例学习(MIL)算法在低标签场景下表现不佳：灵活的模型容易过拟合，而僵化的模型无法适应新任务。实际应用中往往面临标签稀缺的问题，需要一种能够在少量标注数据下快速适应新任务的方法。

Method: 1) 设计基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，支持单次前向传播完成推理；2) 提出多种面向袋结构数据的合成数据生成器，捕获互补的归纳偏置；3) 在混合生成的合成数据上预训练模型，继承各生成器的任务特定优势。

Result: 该方法在12个MIL基准测试上取得了最佳平均性能，超越了需要任务特定训练的监督基线模型，证明了合成数据预训练和上下文学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在多样化的合成数据上预训练上下文学习模型，可以构建出强大的MIL求解器，在推理时无需梯度更新即可从少量标注袋中学习解决新任务，为低标签MIL场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [127] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI-RAN中多控制函数交互干扰问题，提出基于显著性检测的事件检测流水线，通过合成闭环流量生成器验证了从噪声连续遥测数据中可靠恢复潜在依赖结构的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络（如AI-RAN和O-RAN）依赖多个AI控制函数同时优化网络目标，但这些函数间的交互会产生难以检测的干扰。现有方法缺乏可靠且可解释的依赖结构来识别哪些控制参数正在影响哪些网络性能指标。

Method: 1) 构建合成闭环流量生成器，植入已知潜在依赖作为可控测试环境；2) 将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指示器的问题建模为显著性检测问题；3) 设计机器学习流水线区分真实参数-结果关系与背景波动。

Result: 实验表明，当信号与背景波动具有足够分离度时，所提流水线能从噪声连续轨迹中可靠恢复潜在依赖结构；同时揭示了阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向自适应AI-RAN控制系统可解释依赖学习的基础性步骤，为后续依赖管理和控制函数协同优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [128] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多项式预条件层，通过控制权重矩阵奇异值谱来稳定LLM训练，训练后可合并权重且无推理开销，在Llama-1B上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练过程中权重矩阵的条件数可能恶化，导致优化困难和不稳定。现有方法缺乏对权重奇异值谱的直接控制机制。

Method: 提出预条件(PC)层，通过低阶多项式预条件子重新塑形权重矩阵的奇异值谱，确保训练过程中权重条件数稳定。训练后可将预条件权重合并回原始架构，无推理开销。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练中，对AdamW和Muon优化器均显示出优于标准transformer的优势；理论证明了在特定深度线性网络中，均匀限制每层奇异值可保证梯度下降几何收敛到全局最优。

Conclusion: 奇异值谱控制原理是有效的，PC层作为即插即用模块能提升LLM训练稳定性和性能，且不影响推理效率。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [129] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RREDCoT方法，通过利用模型自身近似最优奖励重分配来解决CoT推理中的延迟奖励问题，避免蒙特卡洛方法的高方差和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GRPO算法的推理语言模型微调方法将完整CoT轨迹视为延迟奖励问题，对应标准强化学习中的蒙特卡洛方法，存在高方差问题。虽然通过信用分配进行奖励重分配可以解决该问题，但蒙特卡洛采样的计算开销使其不适用于长上下文的细粒度训练时信用分配。

Method: 提出RREDCoT（Chain of Thoughts的奖励重分配）方法，利用模型自身来近似最优奖励重分配，无需额外生成。该方法避免了蒙特卡洛采样的计算开销，能够进行训练时的信用分配。作者还研究了CoT轨迹分割和状态价值估计等相关构建方面。

Result: 通过与蒙特卡洛采样和多种归因方法的对比研究，验证了RREDCoT方法的优势。同时分析了CoT轨迹分割和状态价值估计等构建要素对重分配效果的影响。

Conclusion: RREDCoT为CoT推理模型的强化学习微调提供了一种高效的奖励重分配解决方案，有效平衡了信用分配的准确性和计算效率，为推理语言模型的训练优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [130] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练(SMT)方法，通过将RNN训练转化为单步记忆转移的监督学习，避免时序反向传播，实现并行训练并改善长距离依赖学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT训练RNN存在两个关键问题：时序计算限制并行性，梯度消失/爆炸导致长距离依赖难以学习。

Method: SMT将RNN训练转化为监督学习任务$(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$，通过训练Transformer编码器获取记忆标签，解耦"记忆什么"与"如何更新记忆"，避免展开RNN。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模任务上，SMT预训练多种RNN架构的表现优于BPTT，能更好捕获长距离依赖。

Conclusion: SMT实现了非线性RNN的并行训练，具有稳定的O(1)梯度路径长度，有望释放构建时序抽象模型的扩展潜力。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [131] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究重复博弈中对手具有适应性时的后悔最小化问题，提出了重复策略后悔（RP-Regret）这一新度量，并开发了三种算法来实现次线性后悔，最终可学习到重复博弈的子博弈完美均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复博弈中，对手能够根据博弈历史做出适应性响应，而标准的在线学习外部后悔度量无法捕捉这种适应性。现有后悔概念在设置上存在诸多约束，无法有效处理玩家反事实推理，因此需要一种原生适用于重复博弈的新后悔度量。

Method: 首先识别了获得次线性RP-Regret的必要条件（涉及比较器策略变化及记忆约束），然后针对策略空间上非凸的RP-Regret优化挑战，提出了三种算法：（i）基于优化预言机的方法；（ii）最小化凸线性化代理RP-Regret的迭代方法；（iii）当对手策略变化缓慢时直接最小化RP-Regret的方法。

Result: 理论证明了在特定条件下可以获得次线性RP-Regret；当所有玩家都最小化RP-Regret（或其线性化变体）时，可以学习到重复博弈的某些子博弈完美均衡；在Stag-Hunt等博弈实验中，最小化该后悔概念可以得到更具合作性、效用更高的解。

Conclusion: RP-Regret作为原生适用于重复博弈的后悔度量，允许更强的比较器和更少约束的对手，能够有效处理适应性对手的反事实推理问题。通过所提出的算法，不仅可以实现后悔最小化，还能学习到更好的博弈均衡，在合作博弈中展现出更高的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [132] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TailLoR，一种基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法，通过固定奇异基作为参考框架学习低秩更新，并引入软谱惩罚减少干扰，将细粒度适应路由到灵活的长尾谱坐标中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习场景中虽有进展，但仍存在改进空间，需要设计更有效的方法来减少对预训练知识的干扰。

Method: 提出TailLoR方法：1) 将预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考框架；2) 在奇异值矩阵上学习低秩更新；3) 引入软谱惩罚，抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新；4) 将细粒度适应路由到灵活的长尾谱坐标中。

Result: 该方法有效减少了持续学习中的干扰，同时保持了对新任务的适应能力，通过利用长尾谱坐标实现了更灵活的参数更新。

Conclusion: TailLoR为持续学习中的参数高效微调提供了一种新的谱分解方法，通过固定奇异基和软谱惩罚机制，成功平衡了对预训练知识的保护和对新任务的适应。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [BIDENT: Heterogeneous Operator-level Mapping for Efficient Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05271)
*Hoseok Kim,Arghadip Das,Soumendu Ghosh,Arnab Raha,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BIDENT提出了一种面向异构边缘SoC的算子级编排框架，通过将算子映射到最合适的处理单元，在多模型并发场景下实现了最高3.42倍加速和48.2%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理框架将整个模型映射到单一处理单元，无法充分利用异构边缘SoC中CPU、GPU、NPU等不同处理单元的性能潜力。新兴架构如状态空间模型、Kolmogorov-Arnold网络和多阶段视觉-语言-动作流水线等，其算子特性差异显著，单一处理单元难以达到最优效果。

Method: BIDENT将算子到处理单元的分配问题建模为加权执行图上的最短路径问题，基于分析得到的执行特征，支持延迟最小化和能耗最小化两种目标。该方法具有模型无关性，统一支持顺序执行、算子级模型内并行以及多模型并发调度，无需依赖特定模型的启发式规则或粗粒度划分。

Result: 在Intel Core Ultra SoC上对10个模型家族进行评估，包括CNN、Transformer、SSM、KAN、脉冲神经网络和多阶段流水线。算子级模型内并行实现最高1.60倍加速；190种多模型组合的几何平均加速达到3.42倍；顺序异构映射实现最高1.58倍加速（几何平均1.09倍）；能耗感知调度在并发场景下平均降低能耗48.2%。

Conclusion: 算子级编排而非模型级映射是充分挖掘下一代边缘AI异构计算能力的关键抽象。通过细粒度的算子调度，BIDENT能够有效利用原本空闲的计算资源，实现显著的性能提升和能耗降低。

Abstract: Modern edge System-on-Chips (SoCs) integrate heterogeneous processing units (PUs) such as CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet current inference stacks map entire models to a single PU, leaving significant performance and energy efficiency on the table. This is exacerbated by emerging architectures such as state-space models (SSMs), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), and multi-stage vision-language-action (VLA) pipelines, whose diverse operator characteristics are not uniformly suited to any single PU. We present BIDENT, a unified operator-level orchestration framework for heterogeneous edge inference that maps individual operators to the most suitable PU based on profiled execution characteristics. BIDENT formulates operator-to-PU assignment as a shortest-path problem over a weighted execution graph, enabling efficient and optimal scheduling under the cost model for both latency- and energy-minimization objectives. Unlike prior work relying on model-specific heuristics or coarse-grained partitioning, BIDENT is model-agnostic and jointly supports sequential execution, intra-model parallelism across independent operators, and multi-model concurrent scheduling in a single formulation. We implement BIDENT on an Intel Core Ultra SoC and evaluate it across 10 model families spanning CNNs, Transformers, SSMs, KANs, spiking networks, and multi-stage pipelines. BIDENT achieves up to 1.60x speedup via intra-model parallelism and a 3.42x geometric mean speedup across 190 multi-model combinations by utilizing otherwise idle compute. Sequential heterogeneous mapping yields more modest gains (up to 1.58x, 1.09x geometric mean), while energy-aware scheduling reduces energy consumption by 48.2% on average in concurrent settings. These results show that operator-level orchestration, not model-level mapping, is the key abstraction for fully exploiting heterogeneity in next-generation edge AI.

</details>


### [134] [MOSAIC: A Workload-Driven Simulation and Design-Space Exploration Framework for Heterogeneous NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05362)
*Arghadip Das,Hoseok Kim,Soomin Lee,Arnab Raha,Deepak A Mathaikutty,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC框架，用于异构NPU的设计空间探索，在7nm节点下实现46.91%的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型架构正在快速多样化，新兴架构（状态空间模型、FFT长卷积、Kolmogorov-Arnold网络、脉冲网络）不再以MAC运算为主导，而是大量依赖向量运算和非MAC原语。现有的同构、MAC中心的NPU对此类工作负载支持不佳，而现有的异构设计仅调整少数粗粒度参数，缺乏支持细粒度异构设计的框架。

Method: 开发MOSAIC分析型模拟器和设计空间探索框架，在tile级别搜索异构设计空间。支持多个维度的异构性：阵列大小、精度、tile类型组合（大Big、小Little、非MAC特殊功能tile）、数据流、稀疏模式、MAC引擎类型以及针对非MAC算子的特殊功能单元。采用分层扫描与遗传算法精化相结合的多种子流水线，生成帕累托最优设计。

Result: 在20个工作负载的测试套件上，MOSAIC找到的最佳通用HPU（约200 mm² Big+Little+Special-Function组合）相比最佳同构基准，在相同面积下实现了46.91%的平均能耗节省。成本模型基于7nm节点校准，并与NVIDIA Deep Learning Accelerator进行了交叉验证。

Conclusion: MOSAIC证明了细粒度异构NPU设计的有效性，为新兴AI架构提供了更好的硬件支持，显著提升了能效表现。该框架填补了现有异构设计工具的空白，支持多维度异构性的联合优化。

Abstract: AI model architectures are diversifying rapidly. Although dense matrix multiplication underlies today's CNNs and transformers, emerging architectures (state-space models, long convolutions via the fast Fourier transform (FFT), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, and spiking networks) are not multiply-accumulate (MAC) dominated; they spend much of their computation on vector and non-MAC primitives that homogeneous, MAC-centric neural processing units (NPUs) serve poorly. This has motivated heterogeneous NPUs (HPUs) built from non-identical tiles. Prior heterogeneous designs vary only one or two coarse knobs (typically MAC precision or array size) and are evaluated on narrow workloads; no existing framework supports fine-grained HPU design, where tiles differ across many architectural dimensions at once. We present MOSAIC, an analytical simulator and design-space-exploration (DSE) framework for HPU microarchitecture design. MOSAIC searches the joint space of tile-level heterogeneity: beyond array size and precision, it varies tile-type composition (large Big, small Little, and non-MAC Special-Function tiles), dataflow, sparsity mode, MAC engine type, and special-function units for non-MAC operators (FFT, spiking-integrate, polynomial). Unlike prior simulators that model a single homogeneous tile type, MOSAIC models non-MAC tiles with their own energy, area, and timing models and maps operators across a mix of tiles with a heterogeneity-aware compiler. A multi-seed pipeline pairing a stratified sweep with genetic-algorithm refinement returns Pareto-optimal designs, with cost models calibrated to a 7 nm node and cross-validated against NVIDIA's Deep Learning Accelerator (NVDLA). Across a 20-workload suite, the best general-purpose HPU found by MOSAIC (~200 mm^2 Big+Little+Special-Function) achieves +46.91% mean iso-area energy savings over the best iso-area homogeneous baseline.

</details>


### [135] [FQA: A Full-Space Quantization-Driven Architecture for Hardware-Efficient Piecewise Approximation of Nonlinear Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05627)
*Chenjun Hao,Feng Yan,Hongbing Pan,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出全空间量化驱动架构（FQA），通过综合考虑截断误差和量化误差，显著降低分段多项式逼近的段数，在Sigmoid函数硬件实现中面积和功耗减少超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分段多项式逼近（PPA）方法在硬件实现中存在局限性：未全面考虑小数位截断误差和量化误差对最优逼近系数的影响，导致无法精确确定和搜索最优系数的完整范围，影响硬件效率和逼近精度。

Method: 提出全空间量化驱动架构（FQA），全面考虑小数位截断误差和量化误差；解耦计算过程中的所有小数字长（FWL）；设计两种硬件实现方案以适应不同资源-性能权衡；提出TBW（目标引导二分窗口）加速方法，加速分段计算和搜索过程，缓解量化空间扩大带来的软件计算时间增加问题。

Result: 相比现有架构，FQA在达到最优最大绝对误差（MAE）的同时显著减少所需段数；Sigmoid函数的硬件设计面积和功耗比最先进PPA架构降低超50%。

Conclusion: FQA实现了硬件高效的分段多项式逼近，提供了完整的可配置硬件部署设计流程，最大化利用现有硬件资源并最小化MAE，为非线性激活函数的高效硬件实现提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a full-space quantization-driven architecture (FQA) for the hardware-efficient piecewise polynomial approximations (PPAs) of nonlinear activation functions. FQA comprehensively considers both fractional-bit truncation error and quantization error that cause the deviation of the optimal approximation coefficients. Crucially, FQA can precisely determine and search the complete range of optimal coefficients. Based on the proposed FQA, we develop two distinct hardware implementation schemes to cater to different resource-performance trade-offs. Furthermore, we decouple all the fractional word lengths (FWLs) involved in the calculation process to enable the exploration of superior hardware architectures. To mitigate the increased software computation time caused by the expanded quantization space, we design an acceleration method named TBW (target-guided bisection window) to expedite the piecewise calculation and searching process. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared to existing architectures, FQA can significantly reduce the number of required segments while achieving the optimal Maximum Absolute Error (MAE). For the hardware design of the Sigmoid function, our approach achieves over 50% reduction in area and power consumption compared to the state-of-the-art PPA architecture. Finally, we present a complete design workflow for deploying PPA on configurable hardware, maximizing the utilization of existing hardware resources and minimizing MAE.

</details>


### [136] [Space-CIM: Enabling Compute-In-Memory Accelerators for Thermally-Constrained Space Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05741)
*Sohan Salahuddin Mugdho,Md. Shahedul Hasan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了太空数据中心的热管理挑战，发现存内计算（CIM）加速器在散热受限的太空环境中相比传统GPU+HBM方案具有更优异的TOPS/W性能和热分布均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求的快速增长导致数据中心建设激增，引发能源和可持续性危机。太空拥有丰富的太阳能资源且发射成本大幅下降，轨道数据中心成为AI计算基础设施扩展的潜在路径。然而太空中的热管理限制（仅依赖辐射冷却需要大面积散热器）对标准液冷/风冷计算机在太空部署构成重大挑战。

Method: 开发了散热器在环协同设计方法论，将系统允许的TOPS（万亿次操作/秒）与太空中的实际散热器冷却能力直接关联。通过热仿真对比分析了具有高带宽内存（HBM）的GPU和新兴存内计算（CIM）加速器在太空热约束下的表现，并系统评估了两者在各种AI工作负载下的性能。

Result: 热仿真显示：分离式GPU芯片和HBM在有限散热器容量下产生严重的热点，需要GPU热节流。相比之下，CIM加速器展现更均匀的热分布，在各种散热器预算范围内TOPS/W性能始终优于GPU。在现实热约束下，CIM在太空部署中具有显著优势。

Conclusion: CIM加速器在太空热约束环境下相比传统GPU方案具有明显优势，更适合轨道数据中心的AI计算部署，为未来太空AI基础设施提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The rapid growth in compute demand from artificial intelligence (AI) has driven a massive surge in data center construction, precipitating an energy and sustainability crisis. Motivated by the abundant solar energy in outer space and the recent sharp reduction in space launch costs, orbital data centers are emerging as a potential pathway for the future scaling of AI compute infrastructure. While the cold background in vacuum seems appealing for cooling, computing systems operating in space without convection ultimately rely on radiative cooling, requiring large-area radiators. Such limitations in thermal management pose a significant challenge for deploying the standard liquid/air-cooled computers in space. In this work, we investigate the impact of the thermal constraints in space on both graphics processing units (GPUs) with high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and the emerging compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators. We develop a radiator-in-the-loop co-design methodology that directly links the permitted system TOPS (terra-operations per second) with the practical radiator cooling capacity in space. Our thermal simulations reveal that the separately located GPU die and HBMs create severe thermal hotspots under limited radiator capacity, necessitating GPU thermal throttling. In contrast, CIM accelerators exhibit a much more uniform heat distribution and consistently outperform GPUs in TOPS/W across a wide range of radiator budgets. We systematically evaluated the performance of CIM and GPU across various AI workloads and demonstrated that CIM has a magnified advantage for deployment in space under realistic thermal constraints.

</details>


### [137] [ITP-STDP: An Intrinsic-Timing Power-of-Two Learning Engine for On-Chip SNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06159)
*Haihang Xia,Xinyu Zhao,Xuecheng Wang,John Goodenough,Charith Abhayaratne,Panagiotis A. Panagiotou,Chunyi Song,Tiantai Deng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ITP-STDP的新型SNN学习算法及其硬件架构，通过算法和硬件层面的优化，在FPGA上实现了4.5倍至219.8倍的能效提升，在ASIC上实现了4.8倍至22.01倍的速度提升，且仅占用先前工作1.2%至3.3%的面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）作为第三代神经网络具有巨大潜力，但大量的突触连接导致片上学习算法在训练过程中需要进行密集的权重更新计算，造成巨大的硬件资源占用和能耗问题。STDP作为SNN中最广泛研究和采用的学习算法之一，其硬件和能耗开销亟待解决。

Method: 提出intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP)算法及其相应的原型学习引擎硬件架构。采用专用的平均场突触漂移模型进行动态分析，并通过不同规模和不同数据集的SNN网络进行验证。该方法在ASIC和FPGA两个平台上实现，并与原始STDP及更复杂的STDP变体进行对比。

Result: 所提出的设计通过算法和硬件层面的优化，消除了STDP的大部分计算开销。在FPGA平台上，能效相比对比设计提升了4.5倍至219.8倍。在ASIC平台上，速度提升了4.8倍至22.01倍，而面积仅占先前工作的1.2%至3.3%。

Conclusion: ITP-STDP成功解决了SNN训练中STDP算法的硬件和能耗开销问题，在保持学习效果的同时实现了显著的能效提升、运行速度提升和硬件资源占用降低，为SNN的硬件实现提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have the potential to emerge as the third generation of neural networks and have attracted increasing attention across a wide range of applications. However, the large number of synaptic connections in SNNs leads to intensive weight-update computation by on-chip learning algorithms during training, resulting in substantial hardware resource utilization and energy consumption. Among existing SNN learning algorithms, spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) is one of the most extensively studied and widely adopted, serving as a fundamental learning component in SNNs. To address the hardware and energy overheads associated with SNN training, this paper presents intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP) and its corresponding prototype learning engine hardware architecture. The proposed design is evaluated through a dedicated mean-field synaptic drift model for dynamical analysis and further validated across SNN networks of different scales and datasets. It is further implemented on both ASIC and FPGA platforms and compared with state-of-the-art approaches, including the original STDP and more complex STDP variants. The results demonstrate superior energy efficiency, higher operating speed, and substantially lower hardware resource utilization, as the proposed design eliminates most of the computational overhead of STDP through both algorithmic and hardware-level optimizations. On the FPGA platform, the proposed design improves energy efficiency by 4.5$\times$ to 219.8$\times$ over the compared designs. On the ASIC platform, the proposed design achieves a 4.8$\times$ to 22.01$\times$ speedup while consuming only 1.2% to 3.3% of the area required by prior works.

</details>


### [138] [Modeling, Optimizing and Exploring Multi-Die FPGA Routing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06421)
*Amirhossein Poolad,Soheil Gholami Shahrouz,Andrew Boutros,Vaughn Betz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过增强VTR工具建立多裸片FPGA架构建模框架，系统探索了2.5D和3D FPGA的裸片间布线架构设计空间，证明合理设计下可实现容量提升而性能损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多裸片FPGA中，裸片堆叠技术的电气物理特性限制了裸片间连接密度和延迟，需要定制化的裸片间布线架构，但缺乏准确通用的建模工具，导致架构设计问题悬而未决。

Method: 增强开源FPGA CAD工具VTR以灵活建模多种多裸片布线架构；改进VPR的布局布线引擎以优化2.5D和3D FPGA；基于7nm工艺和45nm硅中介层进行HSPICE电路建模，系统探索裸片间布线架构设计空间，分析跨裸片技术、连接数量、扇入/扇出、中介层线长对CPD、线长、面积和可布线性等指标的影响。

Result: 3D FPGA相比2D器件可实现最高14%线长缩减和6% CPD改善，在现有10μm节距技术下仍保持可布线性；2.5D FPGA在32%裸片间连通度下仅产生2%线长和4% CPD开销；所有扩展已开源并集成至VTR主分支。

Conclusion: 通过合适的裸片间布线架构设计，2.5D和3D FPGA可以在无明显可布线性或延迟损失的情况下提升容量，为多裸片FPGA架构设计提供了有效工具和设计指导。

Abstract: Die stacking has enabled 2.5D FPGAs by integrating multiple active dice on a passive silicon interposer for improved yield and capacity, and paved the way for 3D architectures that stack active dice directly atop one another. In these multi-die devices, the unique electrical and physical characteristics of the underlying die-stacking technology impose limitations on inter-die connection density and latency, necessitating a bespoke inter-die routing architecture. However, the absence of accurate and versatile modeling tools has left most questions about how to best design the inter-die routing architecture unanswered.
  To address this gap, we enhance the open-source FPGA CAD tool VTR to flexibly model a wide range of multi-die routing architectures, and augment VPR's placement and routing engines to improve optimization for both 2.5D and 3D FPGAs. We perform HSPICE-based circuit modeling of inter-die connections for active dice using a 7 nm process node and a 45 nm silicon interposer across several die-crossing technologies. Using this enhanced framework, we conduct a detailed design space exploration of inter-die routing architecture in 2.5D and 3D FPGAs, characterizing the impact of die-crossing technology, inter-die connection count, fan-in/fan-out, and interposer wire length on critical path delay (CPD), wirelength, area, and routability.
  Our results show that with suitable inter-die routing architectures, 2.5D and 3D FPGAs can increase capacity without significant routability or delay penalties. Specifically, 3D FPGAs achieve up to 14% wirelength reduction and 6% CPD improvement over 2D devices, and remain routable even with existing $10\,μ$m pitch technologies, while 2.5D FPGAs incur only a 2% wirelength and 4% CPD overhead at 32% inter-die connectivity. All extensions are open source and integrated with the VTR master branch.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [RAINO: Anchoring Agents in Reality, A Systematic Review and Conceptual Framework for Realism in Agent-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05167)
*Loïs Vanhée,Melania Borit*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述分析了Agent-Based Modelling中现实主义概念的运用现状，发现该概念定义模糊且缺乏统一框架，据此提出了RAINO框架来系统化地论证模型现实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实主义是Agent-Based Modelling中的核心概念，但目前缺乏系统的理论化阐述。本文旨在通过系统文献综述识别现实主义如何在当前研究中被操作化和论证，以填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，系统梳理现有文献中现实主义的概念定义、操作化方法及其论证方式，在此基础上构建RAINO框架。RAINO框架包含现实锚点（如经验数据、形式理论、专家知识、常识性预期）及其在模型输入和输出中的应用。

Result: 研究发现现实主义在现有文献中普遍定义不清，缺乏一致的概念框架。虽然使用了多种方法来达成和论证现实性，但对于这些方法是否适用于其预期目的的解释通常有限。RAINO框架提供了论证Agent-Based Models现实性的关键结构，拓宽了对现实主义如何被框架化的现有视角。

Conclusion: RAINO框架能够解释为何不同评估者可能以不同方式评估模型的现实性，并展示了这种更广泛的框架化如何引导出显著不同的模型开发方法，为Agent-Based Modelling中现实主义的理论化和实践应用提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Realism is a central yet seemingly under-theorized concept in Agent-Based Modelling. This paper presents a Systematic Literature Review, aiming to identify how realism is currently operationalized and demonstrated. The results show that realism is often poorly defined and lacks a consistent conceptual framework. A wide variety of methods are used to achieve and demonstrate realism, but explanations of whether and why these methods are appropriate for their intended purposes are generally limited. Building on this review, we introduce the Reality Anchor, Input, Output (RAINO) framework. RAINO identifies the key structures used to argue for realism in Agent-Based Models, consisting of Reality Anchors (e.g., empirical data, formal theory, expert knowledge, common-sense expectations) and their application as model Input or Output. RAINO broadens existing perspectives on how realism is framed. It explains why different assessors may evaluate the realism of a model in different ways, and it shows how this broader framing can lead to significantly different approaches to model development.

</details>


### [140] [Ahoy: LLMs Enacting Multiagent Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05390)
*Omkar Joshi,Munindar P. Singh,Amit K. Chopra*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Ahoy系统，利用大语言模型(LLM)创建能够动态选择和执行声明式协议的智能体，实现了无需编程的协议执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统交互协议实现方法会产生特定于所选协议的智能体实现，缺乏通用性。研究动机是如何构建能够执行协议但无需编程的智能智能体。

Method: 开发了Ahoy系统，该系统创建基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体，能够根据用户目标动态选择和执行声明式协议。系统将声明式协议与大语言模型相结合。

Result: 实验证明Ahoy智能体能够正确且智能地执行多个协议，在适应用户目标时甚至可以并发执行多个协议，且无需专门训练。

Conclusion: Ahoy的重要意义在于将声明式协议和大语言模型这两种方法结合在一起，这两种方法都承诺能够改进智能体的知识工程。

Abstract: An interaction protocol formalizes how the agents in a multiagent system interact, which facilitates implementing agents. Existing approaches yield agent implementations specific to the selected protocols. How can we engineer intelligent agents that can enact protocols but are programming-free? Our contribution, Ahoy, addresses this question by creating LLM agents that dynamically select and enact declarative protocols to achieve user goals. We demonstrate that an \ahoy agent can correctly and intelligently enact multiple protocols - concurrently if appropriate to the user goal - without specialized training. Ahoy's significance lies in that it brings together declarative protocols and LLMs, both approaches that promise improved knowledge engineering for agents.

</details>


### [141] [Learning to Contest: Decentralized Robust Fairness in Cooperative MARL via Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06162)
*Can Savcı*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对公平合作多智能体强化学习（MARL）团队易被自私智能体“搭便车”的问题，提出一种去中心化的置换等变交叉注意力策略CAN。其核心在于证明在分级竞争（而非全有或全无）下，最弱势的协作者可通过适度竞争获得比让步更优的收益，从而实现抗剥削。通过对抗性联赛训练（PSRO），CAN在竞争范围内保持了较低的剥削率，效率接近集中式预言机，且在帕累托意义上优于现有公平学习者。同时，研究也揭示了该方法的适用边界：竞争杠杆强时鲁棒性好，弱或零样本迁移到大团队时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大化平等主义福利的公平合作MARL团队存在一个关键缺陷：容易被单一自私智能体剥削，因为自私者可以搭便车，利用公平智能体为提升最弱势者而放弃的剩余。虽然集中式需求分配器可以消除这一问题，但这需要将分配权从智能体手中剥夺。一个开放的问题是：是否存在既保持去中心化又具备鲁棒性的策略？本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先引入“分级竞争”模型，证明当竞争导致资源浪费系数c<1时，最弱势协作者通过竞争而非让步可以获得严格更好的结果（命题1），从而为去中心化抗剥削提供了理论杠杆。基于此，提出CAN策略：一种置换等变的交叉注意力神经网络，通过观察智能体的历史行为推断搭便车者数量，并做出比例性响应：无搭便车者时轮流使用，有时则适度竞争。训练采用策略空间响应预言机（PSRO）框架，让策略与对抗性联赛中的策略博弈，以学习鲁棒策略。

Result: 在竞争范围内，CAN将最优响应剥削率保持在ρ≈1.2-1.5（未保护时ρ=N），低竞争时效率≈1.0，高竞争时效率0.83-0.96，两方面均接近集中式预言机水平。与现有公平MARL学习器相比：GGF/FEN选择让步但易被剥削，SOTO选择全面竞争但造成浪费，而CAN兼具效率与抗剥削性。在多服务器游戏中，CAN保持高效并在帕累托意义上主导公平学习器，但鲁棒性与竞争杠杆强度成正比：杠杆强时鲁棒性好，弱时部分有效，在赢家通吃场景下失效。同时发现了脆弱性：弱杠杆和零样本迁移到更大团队时，高竞争下性能退化。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了去中心化策略在分级竞争条件下能够实现抗剥削性，打破了此前认为必须集中化分配才能解决问题的观念。CAN策略通过推断搭便车者数量并做出比例性响应，在效率与抗剥削两个维度上取得了接近集中式预言机的性能。但研究也明确了适用边界：鲁棒性依赖于竞争杠杆的强度，在杠杆弱或团队规模变化时存在局限。这一发现为公平MARL领域的去中心化机制设计提供了新的理论视角和实践方案，同时提醒研究者在应用时需考虑具体的博弈结构与杠杆条件。

Abstract: Fair cooperative multi-agent RL (MARL) teams maximizing egalitarian welfare are exploitable: a single selfish agent free-rides on the surplus fair agents forgo to raise the worst-off. A centralized need-based allocator removes it, but only by taking allocation out of agents' hands; whether decentralized policies can be robust was left open. We show this futility is an artifact of all-or-nothing contention. Under graded contention (a contested resource delivers $1-c$, wasting $c$), we prove that for any $c<1$ a worst-off cooperator that contests a free-rider strictly improves on yielding, so decentralized leverage exists (Prop. 1). Realizing it is a coordination problem under uncertainty: the number of free-riders is unknown and variable, so any fixed rule is dominated. We introduce CAN, a permutation-equivariant cross-attention policy over agents' observed behaviour that infers the number of free-riders and responds proportionally: turn-taking when none, contesting just enough when some. Trained against an adversarial league (PSRO), CAN keeps best-response exploitability low ($ρ\approx1.2$-$1.5$, vs. $ρ=N$ unprotected) across the contention range, wasting almost nothing at $D=0$ (efficiency $\approx1.0$) and retaining most of it at $D\geq1$ (efficiency 0.83-0.96), approaching the centralized oracle on both axes, no central allocator. Fair-MARL learners fail on complementary axes (GGF/FEN yield and are exploitable, SOTO all-contests and wastes), while CAN is both. On two further games we find clear scope, not blanket generality: CAN stays efficient and Pareto-dominates the fair learners, but its robustness holds only in proportion to the contest leverage: strong on a multi-server game, partial when it weakens, absent under winner-take-all (Prop. 1 fails). We also report its fragilities: weak leverage and zero-shot transfer to larger teams degrade it at high contention.

</details>


### [142] [A Swarm Approach to Public Transit Using On-demand Routing in a Slime-Mold-Inspired Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06189)
*Lindsay Burke,Maxfield Comstock,Jason Graham,Ruth Malenda,Simon Garnier,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粘菌启发的分布式DRT系统，通过动态换乘和协作竞价机制，在城市、郊区和半农村场景下将乘客送达率提高了28%-101%，并减少超过75%的步行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求响应式公交（DRT）在低密度社区表现良好，但存在运营成本高和可靠性低的问题。传统的集中式人工调度方案难以有效解决这些问题，需要更灵活的分布式动态路由方法。

Method: 提出粘菌启发的路由算法，实现多车辆动态路由以最大化网络效能；引入动态换乘方法优化网络效率；通过持续协作竞价过程处理乘客分配和动态换乘；使用OpenStreetMap数据进行多场景仿真测试。

Result: 在郊区、城市和半农村场景下，乘客送达率相比固定网络方法分别提高了28%、49%和101%；所有场景下步行时间减少超过75%。

Conclusion: 基于粘菌启发的分布式DRT系统能够有效提高公交网络效率，显著改善乘客服务体验，为解决传统DRT系统的高成本和低可靠性问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Demand-responsive transit (DRT) is a flexible alternative to traditional, fixed-route mass-transit networks. Although DRT can function well in low-density communities, high operating costs and low reliability are common issues. We propose that these issues can be mitigated by moving from a centralized, manually-scheduled scheme to a distributed system capable of dynamically routing multiple vehicles using a slime-mold-inspired routing algorithm to maximize network effectiveness. We additionally introduce the method of dynamic transfers to further optimize transit network efficiency. All passenger allocation and dynamic transfers are handled via a continual cooperative bidding process by the buses. In this paper, we present simulated results for a swarm-driven transit network in suburban, urban, and semi-rural scenarios, using map networks pulled from OpenStreetMap.
